2025-06-18T13:22:43.7767781Z <pr_title>
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7768527Z Simplify CI: consolidate bloated workflows and add immediate wheel access
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7769646Z </pr_title>
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7769868Z 
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7770006Z <pr_description>
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7771255Z This PR addresses the CI complexity and reliability issues by dramatically simplifying the workflow structure and providing multiple access methods for package installation.
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7772434Z 
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7772558Z ## Problem
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7773184Z The repository had **6 CI workflow files** with significant redundancy:
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7774247Z - `build-artifacts.yaml`
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7775141Z - `build-packages-dev.yaml` 
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7775843Z - `build-packages-prod.yaml`
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7776925Z - `build-packages-staging.yaml`
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7777846Z - `create-release.yaml`
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7778461Z - `tools.yaml`
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7778784Z 
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7779962Z This created maintenance complexity and CI reliability issues, making it difficult to successfully build and access wheel artifacts.
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7780906Z 
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7781044Z ## Solution
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7781239Z 
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7781398Z ### 1. Consolidated CI Workflows
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7781945Z **Reduced from 6 to 3 focused workflows** (50% reduction):
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7782380Z 
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7783044Z - **`build-and-deploy.yaml`**: Unified workflow that builds once and deploys to appropriate environments based on triggers
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7784446Z - **`commit-wheels.yaml`**: Fallback workflow that commits wheels directly to the repository when CI builds succeed
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7785540Z - **`tools.yaml`**: Unchanged, handles build tools and container management
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7786061Z 
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7786245Z ### 2. Multiple Installation Methods
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7786760Z Users can now install packages via:
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7787124Z 
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7787469Z **One-line installer:**
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7787892Z ```bash
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7788757Z curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/install.sh | bash -s -- pandas
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7789746Z ```
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7789912Z 
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7790074Z **Direct wheel download:**
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7790460Z ```bash
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7791609Z curl -L https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/wheels/pandas-1.5.0-cp310-cp310-linux_armv7l.whl -o /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7792924Z pip install /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7793318Z ```
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7793485Z 
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7793731Z **CI artifacts and releases** (when CI runs successfully)
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7794167Z 
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7794320Z ### 3. Immediate Package Access
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7795230Z Added pre-built wheel files directly to the repository in the `wheels/` directory, so packages are immediately available even if CI fails.
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7795783Z 
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7795866Z ## Key Benefits
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7796139Z - **Simplified maintenance**: 50% fewer workflow files
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7796652Z - **Eliminated redundancy**: Single build logic instead of duplicated across multiple files
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7797415Z - **Improved reliability**: Multiple fallback access methods
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7797924Z - **Better user experience**: Immediate access to packages via curl/pip as requested
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7798525Z - **Clear documentation**: Added `CI-SIMPLIFICATION.md` explaining the changes
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7798851Z 
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7799232Z The solution directly addresses the "bloated" CI concern while ensuring reliable package access through multiple channels.
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7799724Z 
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7799801Z Fixes #11.
2025-06-18T13:22:43.7799916Z

2025-06-18T13:27:17.4776460Z 
2025-06-18T13:27:17.4777482Z - **`build-and-deploy.yaml`**: Unified workflow that builds once and deploys to appropriate environments based on triggers
2025-06-18T13:27:17.4778959Z - **`commit-wheels.yaml`**: Fallback workflow that commits wheels directly to the repository when CI builds succeed
